Critical dates shall be as displayed on the GeM bid page. Any amendment/corrigendum issued before bid closing shall form part of the tender document.

===== PAGE 3 =====
SECTION II - NOTICE INVITING TENDER
1. Invitation
The National Public Infrastructure Agency (NPIA) invites bids from eligible suppliers/integrators for supply, installation, integration, testing, commissioning and maintenance of intelligent learning infrastructure at 80 government residential schools.
2. Pre-bid Conference and Clarifications
• A pre-bid conference may be held online. Participation is optional unless otherwise specified in the GeM bid.
• Prospective bidders shall submit clarification requests within the time window published in the tender information summary.
• Responses and corrigenda shall be uploaded through the procurement portal and bidders are responsible for considering the latest version of the tender.
3. Submission of Bids
• Bids shall be submitted electronically through GeM by the prescribed deadline.
• The technical bid shall contain all eligibility, qualification, technical and documentary submissions requested in this tender.
• The financial bid shall contain item-wise rates in the prescribed Bill of Quantity/price schedule.
• Bidders shall not alter the substantive provisions of the tender document while completing forms.
4. Rights of Procuring Entity
• NPIA may accept or reject bids in accordance with the tender conditions.
• NPIA may issue amendments/corrigenda before the bid submission deadline.
• The issue of this tender does not constitute an obligation to award a contract.

2. General Conditions for Bid Preparation
• The bidder shall study the complete tender, including all annexures and cross-referenced forms.
• Documents shall be legible and signed/digitally signed where required.
• Any material deviation from a mandatory technical requirement shall be declared in the technical compliance form.
• A bidder shall be responsible for the completeness and authenticity of submitted information.
3. Bid Validity and Withdrawal
The bid shall remain valid for 120 days. Withdrawal or modification after bid closing shall be governed by the procurement portal and tender conditions.
4. Conflict of Interest
Bidders shall disclose actual or potential conflicts of interest affecting impartial participation. The procuring entity may seek clarification or supporting documentation.

SECTION IV - PUBLIC PROCUREMENT POLICIES
1. Make in India / Local Content
For the offered intelligent display, UPS and network equipment, the bidder shall state the percentage of local content and identify the location(s) of local value addition. For this synthetic tender, a minimum local-content level of 50% is required for the primary equipment package to qualify as Class-I local supply for the purpose stated in this tender.

The compliance matrix in this tender intentionally separates conditions that can be checked from structured evidence from conditions that require officer review. A declaration or document submission does not, by itself, constitute independent verification.
Cross-reference: Technical responses shall be provided in Form 4 - Technical Specifications Compliance & Deviations. Qualification evidence shall be provided in Form 5 - Qualification Criteria Compliance.

===== PAGE 8 =====
SECTION VI - SCOPE OF WORK
The successful bidder shall provide an end-to-end package for 80 government residential schools, including supply, transportation, installation, integration, testing, commissioning, documentation and training.
• Site survey and deployment planning for each school.
• Supply and installation of intelligent interactive displays, UPS units and network connectivity equipment.
• Electrical and structured cabling work necessary for safe installation.
• Network configuration, device enrollment and baseline security hardening.
• System testing and site acceptance testing.
• Teacher/user orientation and handover documentation.
• Warranty support, defect rectification and periodic preventive maintenance.
• Submission of as-built documentation, asset registers and serial-number lists.
Site execution shall comply with the implementation schedule and acceptance criteria on subsequent pages. The bidder remains responsible for coordinating all equipment and installation activities within the awarded scope.

===== PAGE 9 =====

SECTION XII - COMMERCIAL, SECURITY AND CONTRACT CONDITIONS
• Performance security: 5% of awarded contract value unless otherwise stated in the contract.
• Payment: milestone-based payment against delivery, installation, commissioning and acceptance certificates.
• Prices: firm during the implementation period unless an approved variation clause applies.
• Insurance and safe custody of materials remain the bidder responsibility until accepted at site.
• Subcontracting of material scope shall require prior written approval and shall not relieve the bidder of contractual responsibility.
